| 2026-10-13 | NOTE (orchestrator) | — | Arbitrary unit h for Root-F: reduction to e₀ by a rotation Q (`RotateUnit.lean`) gives Z_h(g) = Z_{e₀}(Qᵀg) and T^h(x,X;g) = Q·T^{e₀}(x,X;Qᵀg), but the frozen domain {∑_y ∑_i ‖g y i‖ < 1} is an entrywise ℓ¹ ball, not rotation invariant: Qᵀ can enlarge it by up to √N. So either the e₀ chain must give analyticity and the identity for ∑‖g‖₁ < √N (source radius R instead of 1 along the chain), or the step must be done at a general constant h. To decide before the root assembly. |
